# Preliminary Androlinzberg AOSP source manifest.
# Format: <kind> <source> <path>
#   D = all Git-tracked entries recursively; F = exact tracked file
# Source: aosp is the Repo checkout initialized at android-16.0.0_r1.
# Derived from build/gen/{island,skia,protobuf}_sources.zig,
# build/java_sources.zig, the generator input lists under tools/, and direct
# AOSP paths in build.zig/build/*.zig and tools/*.sh.
# Directory compaction uses >=80% coverage by tracked count and bytes,
# plus cohesive trees whose build consumes broad include/resource closure.
# external/skia is intentionally whole and is consumed directly by the build.
# Frameworks-base patches 0001-0002 are deliberately omitted because they adapt
# HWUI to the former stock-Skia tree rather than this AOSP revision.
# Generated out/host aprotoc is not a source input; protoc is built by Zig.
# The archive generator must apply the selected frameworks-base,
# incremental_delivery, minikin, and system/libbase patches before packaging,
# then revert them.
# Rules: D=129 F=6411 total=6540
